What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: I feel like people at work are secretly plotting against me, even when they tell me they’re not. It's just so stressful because I can feel in my gut that they are trying to get me fired, no matter what they say to reassure me.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself having similar suspicions about other people in your life, like family or friends, or is this feeling strictly limited to your coworkers?\n\nPatient: No, it's really just at the restaurant with the other servers and some of the regulars. I don't feel that way about my husband or my friends, but I've been pulling away from them lately just because I feel completely exhausted and don't have the energy to keep up with anyone.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices, seen things, or experienced other sensations that other people don't seem to notice?\n\nPatient: I haven't seen anything unusual, but I do hear voices pretty often, especially when I'm at work or late at night. I'll hear my name or a couple of voices whispering that I'm screwing things up and don't deserve my job, even when I can see no one is near me and the kitchen is quiet. It's really confusing and it worries me.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this exhausted and pulling away from others, and do these low moods always happen at the same time you hear the voices?\n\nPatient: The exhaustion and pulling away from my friends and hobbies have been going on for a little over a year, starting around when the restaurant changed ownership. The whispering voices and feeling like people are plotting against me are much more recent, only starting regularly about four months ago. So the low energy and withdrawal definitely started long before the voices did.\n\nDoctor: During these past four months, have you had periods lasting at least two weeks where you heard the voices or felt plotted against, but your mood actually felt relatively normal or okay?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure, to be honest. I feel like I've just been generally overwhelmed and down this entire time, so I haven't really noticed if there were times when my mood felt normal. It's all just kind of blurred together lately.\n\nDoctor: Besides feeling exhausted and pulling away, have you been experiencing a nearly constant sad or empty mood, or a total loss of interest in almost all of your usual activities?\n\nPatient: Yes, I have completely lost interest in the things I used to love, like reading or taking sunrise walks on the beach, because they just feel like too much effort now and don't bring me any joy. Instead, I mostly just lie on the couch or sit in the backyard staring at the plants. I've even stopped returning calls from friends and let my husband handle all of our social stuff because I just can't keep up.\n\nDoctor: Before this past year when you started feeling so exhausted and down, had you ever experienced hearing whispering voices or feeling like people were plotting against you?\n\nPatient: No, I've never experienced anything like this before in my life. The whispering voices and the feeling that people are plotting against me are completely new to me, only starting about four months ago. Before that, starting about a year and a half ago, it was just my general struggles at work and pulling away from my routines.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are being broadcast aloud so others can hear them, or that someone else is putting thoughts into your mind?\n\nPatient: No, I've never experienced anything like that. I don't feel like my thoughts are being broadcasted for others to hear, and I've never felt like anyone else was putting thoughts into my head.\n\nDoctor: During this past year of feeling so down, have you also been struggling with feelings of worthlessness, excessive guilt, or thoughts of death or suicide?"

I definitely feel overwhelmed and down about all my struggles at work, but I haven't experienced those specific feelings.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your sleep patterns or your appetite since you started feeling so exhausted and down a year ago?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed any major changes in my sleep patterns or my appetite. Mostly, I just feel completely exhausted, overwhelmed, and unmotivated all the time. It feels like even the simplest things take way too much effort.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any difficulty concentrating, focusing on tasks, or making simple decisions over this past year?"

It's really confusing and it worries me.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this exhausted and pulling away from others, and do these low moods always happen at the same time you hear the voices?\n\nPatient: The exhaustion and pulling away from my friends and hobbies have been going on for a little over a year, starting around when the restaurant changed ownership. The whispering voices and feeling like people are plotting against me are much more recent, only starting regularly about four months ago. So the low energy and withdrawal definitely started long before the voices did.\n\nDoctor: During these past four months, have you had periods lasting at least two weeks where you heard the voices or felt plotted against, but your mood actually felt relatively normal or okay?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure, to be honest. I feel like I've just been generally overwhelmed and down this entire time, so I haven't really noticed if there were times when my mood felt normal. It's all just kind of blurred together lately.\n\nDoctor: Besides feeling exhausted and pulling away, have you been experiencing a nearly constant sad or empty mood, or a total loss of interest in almost all of your usual activities?\n\nPatient: Yes, I have completely lost interest in the things I used to love, like reading or taking sunrise walks on the beach, because they just feel like too much effort now and don't bring me any joy. Instead, I mostly just lie on the couch or sit in the backyard staring at the plants. I've even stopped returning calls from friends and let my husband handle all of our social stuff because I just can't keep up.\n\nDoctor: Before this past year when you started feeling so exhausted and down, had you ever experienced hearing whispering voices or feeling like people were plotting against you?\n\nPatient: No, I've never experienced anything like this before in my life. The whispering voices and the feeling that people are plotting against me are completely new to me, only starting about four months ago. Before that, starting about a year and a half ago, it was just my general struggles at work and pulling away from my routines.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts are being broadcast aloud so others can hear them, or that someone else is putting thoughts into your mind?\n\nPatient: No, I've never experienced anything like that. I don't feel like my thoughts are being broadcasted for others to hear, and I've never felt like anyone else was putting thoughts into my head.\n\nDoctor: During this past year of feeling so down, have you also been struggling with feelings of worthlessness, excessive guilt, or thoughts of death or suicide?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had any thoughts of suicide or self-harm, and I don't feel worthless or excessively guilty. I definitely feel overwhelmed and down about all my struggles at work, but I haven't experienced those specific feelings.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your sleep patterns or your appetite since you started feeling so exhausted and down a year ago?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed any major changes in my sleep patterns or my appetite. Mostly, I just feel completely exhausted, overwhelmed, and unmotivated all the time. It feels like even the simplest things take way too much effort.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any difficulty concentrating, focusing on tasks, or making simple decisions over this past year?\n\nPatient: Yes, I've really struggled to focus and make decisions this past year; I make more mistakes at work, fall behind on new procedures, and sometimes literally freeze up at the server station just staring at the tickets. It's gotten so hard to keep up with everything that I've had to let my husband handle all of our basic household decisions, bills, and errands.\n\nDoctor: Have your family or coworkers noticed any changes in how you speak, such as having trouble finding your words, jumping from topic to topic, or saying things that don't make sense to them?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I've had any trouble finding my words or jumping from topic to topic. No one at work or my husband has ever mentioned anything like that to me."
  }

It's gotten so hard to keep up with everything that I've had to let my husband handle all of our basic household decisions, bills, and errands.\n\nDoctor: Have your family or coworkers noticed any changes in how you speak, such as having trouble finding your words, jumping from topic to topic, or saying things that don't make sense to them?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I've had any trouble finding my words or jumping from topic to topic.
